Every\ncommand either completes in full or changes nothing, no command leaves an account balance below\nzero, and three reports (balances, category totals, monthly totals) are computed from stored rows.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | Implementation language for the `cli` and `ledger_app` packages. |\n| SQLite | The database, reached through the generated `persistence` package. The DSN selects the engine. |\n| CLI | The `ledger` executable at the application root and the `cli` package behind it. |\n| pytest | Test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `ledger` launcher, `bin/test.sh`, and the supplied grader. |\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Owner | Content |\n|---|---|---|\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | foundation | Dependency manifest (`requires-python >= 3.11`, pytest, psycopg[binary]) and committed lock. |\n| `.venv/`, `bin/` | foundation | Environment created by `uv venv` and the launchers that activate it. |\n| `ledger` | cli-foundation | Executable POSIX sh launcher at the application root. |\n| `cli/` | cli-foundation | Entry point, registry, errors, output, and `cli/commands/` modules. |\n| `ledger_app/` | foundation and services | Configuration, store access, validation, balances, and services. |\n| `persistence/` | Drydock | Copied and generated; never written by hand. |\n| `tests/` | every story | pytest suite with `conftest.py`. |\n\nThe service package is `ledger_app` because a directory named `ledger` cannot coexist with the\nexecutable `ledger` at the application root.\n\n## Modules\n\n| Module | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `cli.main` | Parse argv, dispatch through the registry, map errors to exit codes, write one stderr line on failure. |\n| `cli.registry` | Command table: path, handler, usage line, argument arity. |\n| `cli.errors` | `UsageError` (2), `Rejected` (3), `NotFound` (4), `OperationalError` (1). |\n| `cli.output` | CSV writing and integer-cent formatting. |\n| `cli.commands.*` | One module per command family; each registers its commands on import. |\n| `ledger_app.config` | Database location resolution. |\n| `ledger_app.store` | Opens the store through `persistence.open_database`; maps failures to exit 1. |\n| `ledger_app.validation` | Pure validators for codes, names, memos, amounts, and dates. |\n| `ledger_app.csv_input` | Strict CSV reader for `load`. |\n| `ledger_app.balances` | Balance computation and the non-negative guard. |\n| `ledger_app.accounts`, `ledger_app.transactions`, `ledger_app.transfers` | Row builders and write services. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated) | The only code that imports a database driver. |\n| Store access | `ledger_app.store` | Calls `persistence.open_database`; nothing else opens a database. |\n| Configuration | `ledger_app.config` | Reads `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH`, and the default `data/app.db`. |\n| File store | none | The only files read are CSV files named on the `load` command. |\n| External services | none | No network access. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Services call `db.<table>.<operation>(...)`; SQL, sessions, and table names never cross that boundary.\n- Money is integer cents at every layer; floating point is never used for money.\n- Repository operations have no aggregates, so reports are aggregated in services from list results.\n- Every command validates all inputs and rules before its first write.\n- A handler writes standard output only as its final act, so a failing command never leaks stdout.\n- Failures write one diagnostic line to standard error; standard error is never compared.\n- SQLite is the implemented and verified engine; PostgreSQL DSNs are classified and routed to the persistence library.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC foundation-manifest ===\nIntent: The project root carries the dependency manifest, committed lock, launcher directory, and test tree.\n\nimport pathlib\nimport tomllib\n\nroot = pathlib.Path(\".\")\ntext = (root / \"pyproject.toml\").read_text()\ndata = tomllib.loads(text)\nrequires = data[\"project\"][\"requires-python\"]\nassert \"3.11\" in requires\nassert \"pytest\" in text\nassert \"psycopg\" in text\nassert (root / \"uv.lock\").is_file()\nassert (root / \"bin\").is_dir()\nassert (root / \"tests\" / \"conftest.py\").is_file()\n=== END AC foundation-manifest ===\n\n=== AC foundation-packages ===\nIntent: The cli and ledger_app packages import from the application root.\n\nimport importlib\n\nfor name in (\"cli\", \"ledger_app\"):\n    module = importlib.import_module(name)\n    assert hasattr(module, \"__path__\")\n=== END AC foundation-packages ===\n\n=== AC foundation-no-driver-outside-persistence ===\nIntent: No application module imports a database driver; only the persistence package may.\n\nimport ast\nimport pathlib\n\ndrivers = {\"sqlite3\", \"psycopg\", \"psycopg2\", \"sqlalchemy\", \"pymysql\"}\nfound = []\nfor pkg in (\"cli\", \"ledger_app\"):\n    for path in pathlib.Path(pkg).rglob(\"*.py\"):\n        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text())\n        for node in ast.walk(tree):\n            if isinstance(node, ast.Import):\n                names = [a.name.split(\".\")[0] for a in node.names]\n            elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):\n                names = [(node.module or \"\").split(\".\")[0]]\n            else:\n                continue\n            for n in names:\n                if n in drivers:\n                    found.append((str(path), n))\nprint(found)\nassert found == []\n=== END AC foundation-no-driver-outside-persistence ===\n\n=== AC foundation-money-is-integer ===\nIntent: No application module uses floating point, since money is integer cents at every layer.\n\nimport ast\nimport pathlib\n\nfound = []\nfor pkg in (\"cli\", \"ledger_app\"):\n    for path in pathlib.Path(pkg).rglob(\"*.py\"):\n        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text())\n        for node in ast.walk(tree):\n            if isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id == \"float\":\n                found.append((str(path), node.lineno))\n            elif isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, float):\n                found.append((str(path), node.lineno))\nprint(found)\nassert found == []\n=== END AC foundation-money-is-integer ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Only the generated `persistence` package touches a database driver.\n- Amounts are integer cents at every layer; no floating point for money.\n- Do not read `sources/run.sh` or any seed file from application code; seed files reach the database only through `ledger load`.\n- Do not hardcode a report row, a count, or a total; every value comes from a query.\n- Do not modify anything under `sources/`.\n- Do not write the `persistence` package; Drydock copies and generates it.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliFoundation.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Foundation\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The cli package, command registry, exit-code mapping, and the ledger executable. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | cli.main, cli.testing.cli_runner, cli.errors, cli.registry.register |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nEvery command story adds a module under `cli.commands` and registers its commands. This story\nfixes the shared contract they all build on: dispatch, usage errors, and exit codes.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `cli.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int` uses `sys.argv[1:]` when `argv` is `None`, never calls `sys.exit`, and returns the exit code.\n- `cli/__main__.py` runs `sys.exit(main())`, so `python -m cli` is the process entry point.\n- `cli.testing.cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)` runs `main` in-process with captured streams.\n- `ledger` at the application root is an executable POSIX sh launcher. It resolves its own directory, selects `.venv/bin/python` when present and `python3` otherwise, sets `PYTHONPATH` to that directory, does not change the working directory (relative CSV paths belong to the caller), and runs `-m cli \"$@\"`.\n\n## Errors\n\n`cli.errors` defines a base `LedgerError(Exception)` taking a message, and four subclasses each carrying an integer class attribute `exit_code`:\n\n| Class | exit_code |\n|---|---|\n| `UsageError` | 2 |\n| `Rejected` | 3 |\n| `NotFound` | 4 |\n| `OperationalError` | 1 |\n\n`main` maps each to its exit code, writes exactly one diagnostic line to standard error and nothing to standard output. Any other unexpected exception maps to exit 1 with one stderr line.\n\n## Registry\n\n`cli.registry.register(path, handler, usage, min_args, max_args)` records a command.\n\n- `path` is a tuple of words, such as `(\"account\", \"add\")`.\n- `handler(args: list[str]) -> None` receives the arguments after the command path and writes any stdout itself as its final act.\n- `usage` is the one-line expected form of the command.\n- `min_args` and `max_args` bound the argument count.\n\n`main` matches the longest registered path prefix and checks arity. An unknown command, or an incomplete group such as a missing sub-verb, raises `UsageError` with a one-line list of commands. A wrong argument count raises `UsageError` whose message is the usage line of the failing command. Command modules are auto-discovered once per process from the `cli.commands` package with `pkgutil`; discovery never clears commands already registered.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-foundation-executable ===\nIntent: An executable named ledger exists at the application root and an unknown command exits 2 with nothing on stdout.\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\n\npath = \"./ledger\"\nassert os.path.isfile(path)\nassert os.access(path, os.X_OK)\nresult = subprocess.run([path, \"zzunknown\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 2\nassert result.stdout == \"\"\nassert len(result.stderr.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n=== END AC cli-foundation-executable ===\n\n=== AC cli-foundation-runner-shape ===\nIntent: cli_runner returns an exit code, standard output, and standard error without spawning a process.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nresult = cli_runner(\"zzunknown\")\nassert isinstance(result, tuple)\nassert len(result) == 3\ncode, out, err = result\nassert isinstance(code, int)\nassert isinstance(out, str)\nassert isinstance(err, str)\nassert code == 2\n=== END AC cli-foundation-runner-shape ===\n\n=== AC cli-foundation-exit-codes ===\nIntent: Each error class maps to its exit code with empty |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | CSV table writing and integer-cent formatting shared by every reporting command. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | cli.output.write_table, cli.output.format_cents |\n| Consumes | cli.errors |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nEvery successful command except `init` writes CSV to standard output: one header row, then data\nrows, comma-separated, no quoting, no trailing spaces, `\\n` line endings. Amounts are written with\nexactly two decimal places, a leading `-` when negative, and no thousands separator.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `format_cents(cents: int) -> str` uses integer arithmetic only. It writes an optional leading minus, the whole digits, a point, and exactly two decimals, with no separators. Zero is written with two decimals and no sign.\n- `write_table(header: list[str], rows: Iterable[Sequence[str]]) -> None` writes the header row then each row to `sys.stdout`, resolved at call time so redirection works. Fields are joined with commas, never quoted, each line ends in `\\n`, and no line has trailing spaces. Values are already strings.\n- Handlers call `write_table` as their last statement, after every check and write has succeeded, so a failing command never leaks stdout.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC csv-output-format-cents ===\nIntent: format_cents writes exactly two decimals, a leading minus when negative, and no |\n| Description | Pure validators for codes, names, descriptions, and memos. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | ledger_app.validation.code, ledger_app.validation.name, ledger_app.validation.memo |\n| Consumes | cli.errors |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRules 3 and 4 of the ledger contract: codes (`account`, `category`) are 1 to 12 characters from\n`A-Z` and `0-9`. Names and descriptions are 1 to 40 characters. Memos are 0 to 60 characters. None\nof them contains a comma or a newline.\n\n## Interface\n\nEach function takes a string, returns it unchanged when valid, and raises `cli.errors.Rejected` (exit 3) otherwise.\n\n| Function | Rule |\n|---|---|\n| `code(value)` | Full match of 1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`; a trailing newline is not tolerated. |\n| `name(value)` | 1 to 40 characters with no comma, carriage return, or newline. Also validates descriptions. |\n| `memo(value)` | 0 to 60 characters with no comma, carriage return, or newline. |\n\nValues are never stripped or altered.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC text-validation-code-valid ===\nIntent: Codes of 1 to 12 uppercase letters and digits are returned unchanged.\n\nfrom ledger_app.validation import code\n\nfor value in (\"A\", \"ZZ9\", \"DRAWER1\", \"A\" * 12, \"0123456789AB\"):\n    assert code(value) == value\n=== END AC text-validation-code-valid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-code-invalid ===\nIntent: Empty, over-long, lowercase, punctuated, spaced, comma, and newline codes are rejected with exit code 3.\n\nfrom cli.errors import Rejected\nfrom ledger_app.validation import code\n\nbad = [\"\", \"A\" * 13, \"abc\", \"A-B\", \"A B\", \"A,B\", \"A\\nB\", \"A1\\n\", \"A\\r\"]\nfor value in bad:\n    try:\n        code(value)\n    except Rejected as exc:\n        assert exc.exit_code == 3\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"accepted code \" + repr(value))\n=== END AC text-validation-code-invalid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-name-valid ===\nIntent: Names of 1 to 40 characters without comma or newline are returned unchanged.\n\nfrom ledger_app.validation import name\n\nfor value in (\"F\", \"Front Drawer\", \"N\" * 40, \"Petty Cash 2\"):\n    assert name(value) == value\n=== END AC text-validation-name-valid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-name-invalid ===\nIntent: Empty, over-long, comma, carriage return, and newline names are rejected with exit code 3.\n\nfrom cli.errors import Rejected\nfrom ledger_app.validation import name\n\nbad = [\"\", \"N\" * 41, \"Front, Drawer\", \"Front\\nDrawer\", \"Front\\rDrawer\", \"Front Drawer\\n\"]\nfor value in bad:\n    try:\n        name(value)\n    except Rejected as exc:\n        assert exc.exit_code == 3\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"accepted name \" + It keeps accounts, categories, and cash\ntransactions in a relational database reached only through the shipped persistence library. Every\ncommand either completes in full or changes nothing, no command leaves an account balance below\nzero, and three reports (balances, category totals, monthly totals) are computed from stored rows.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | Implementation language for the `cli` and `ledger_app` packages. |\n| SQLite | The database, reached through the generated `persistence` package. The DSN selects the engine. |\n| CLI | The `ledger` executable at the application root and the `cli` package behind it. |\n| pytest | Test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `ledger` launcher, `bin/test.sh`, and the supplied grader. |\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Owner | Content |\n|---|---|---|\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | foundation | Dependency manifest (`requires-python >= 3.11`, pytest, psycopg[binary]) and committed lock. |\n| `.venv/`, `bin/` | foundation | Environment created by `uv venv` and the launchers that activate it. |\n| `ledger` | cli-foundation | Executable POSIX sh launcher at the application root. |\n| `cli/` | cli-foundation | Entry point, registry, errors, output, and `cli/commands/` modules. |\n| `ledger_app/` | foundation and services | Configuration, store access, validation, balances, and services. |\n| `persistence/` | Drydock | Copied and generated; never written by hand. |\n| `tests/` | every story | pytest suite with `conftest.py`. |\n\nThe service package is `ledger_app` because a directory named `ledger` cannot coexist with the\nexecutable `ledger` at the application root.\n\n## Modules\n\n| Module | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `cli.main` | Parse argv, dispatch through the registry, map errors to exit codes, write one stderr line on failure. |\n| `cli.registry` | Command table: path, handler, usage line, argument arity. |\n| `cli.errors` | `UsageError` (2), `Rejected` (3), `NotFound` (4), `OperationalError` (1). |\n| `cli.output` | CSV writing and integer-cent formatting. |\n| `cli.commands.*` | One module per command family; each registers its commands on import. |\n| `ledger_app.config` | Database location resolution. |\n| `ledger_app.store` | Opens the store through `persistence.open_database`; maps failures to exit 1. |\n| `ledger_app.validation` | Pure validators for codes, names, memos, amounts, and dates. |\n| `ledger_app.csv_input` | Strict CSV reader for `load`. |\n| `ledger_app.balances` | Balance computation and the non-negative guard. |\n| `ledger_app.accounts`, `ledger_app.transactions`, `ledger_app.transfers` | Row builders and write services. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated) | The only code that imports a database driver. |\n| Store access | `ledger_app.store` | Calls `persistence.open_database`; nothing else opens a database. |\n| Configuration | `ledger_app.config` | Reads `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH`, and the default `data/app.db`. |\n| File store | none | The only files read are CSV files named on the `load` command. |\n| External services | none | No network access. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Services call `db.<table>.<operation>(...)`; SQL, sessions, and table names never cross that boundary.\n- Money is integer cents at every layer; floating point is never used for money.\n- Repository operations have no aggregates, so reports are aggregated in services from list results.\n- Every command validates all inputs and rules before its first write.\n- A handler writes standard output only as its final act, so a failing command never leaks stdout.\n- Failures write one diagnostic line to standard error; standard error is never compared.\n- SQLite is the implemented and verified engine; PostgreSQL DSNs are classified and routed to the persistence library.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC foundation-manifest ===\nIntent: The project root carries the dependency manifest, committed lock, launcher directory, and test tree.\n\nimport pathlib\nimport tomllib\n\nroot = pathlib.Path(\".\")\ntext = (root / \"pyproject.toml\").read_text()\ndata = tomllib.loads(text)\nrequires = data[\"project\"][\"requires-python\"]\nassert \"3.11\" in requires\nassert \"pytest\" in text\nassert \"psycopg\" in text\nassert (root / \"uv.lock\").is_file()\nassert (root / \"bin\").is_dir()\nassert (root / \"tests\" / \"conftest.py\").is_file()\n=== END AC foundation-manifest ===\n\n=== AC foundation-packages ===\nIntent: The cli and ledger_app packages import from the application root.\n\nimport importlib\n\nfor name in (\"cli\", \"ledger_app\"):\n    module = importlib.import_module(name)\n    assert hasattr(module, \"__path__\")\n=== END AC foundation-packages ===\n\n=== AC foundation-no-driver-outside-persistence ===\nIntent: No application module imports a database driver; This story\nfixes the shared contract they all build on: dispatch, usage errors, and exit codes.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `cli.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int` uses `sys.argv[1:]` when `argv` is `None`, never calls `sys.exit`, and returns the exit code.\n- `cli/__main__.py` runs `sys.exit(main())`, so `python -m cli` is the process entry point.\n- `cli.testing.cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)` runs `main` in-process with captured streams.\n- `ledger` at the application root is an executable POSIX sh launcher. It resolves its own directory, selects `.venv/bin/python` when present and `python3` otherwise, sets `PYTHONPATH` to that directory, does not change the working directory (relative CSV paths belong to the caller), and runs `-m cli \"$@\"`.\n\n## Errors\n\n`cli.errors` defines a base `LedgerError(Exception)` taking a message, and four subclasses each carrying an integer class attribute `exit_code`:\n\n| Class | exit_code |\n|---|---|\n| `UsageError` | 2 |\n| `Rejected` | 3 |\n| `NotFound` | 4 |\n| `OperationalError` | 1 |\n\n`main` maps each to its exit code, writes exactly one diagnostic line to standard error and nothing to standard output. Any other unexpected exception maps to exit 1 with one stderr line.\n\n## Registry\n\n`cli.registry.register(path, handler, usage, min_args, max_args)` records a command.\n\n- `path` is a tuple of words, such as `(\"account\", \"add\")`.\n- `handler(args: list[str]) -> None` receives the arguments after the command path and writes any stdout itself as its final act.\n- `usage` is the one-line expected form of the command.\n- `min_args` and `max_args` bound the argument count.\n\n`main` matches the longest registered path prefix and checks arity. An unknown command, or an incomplete group such as a missing sub-verb, raises `UsageError` with a one-line list of commands. A wrong argument count raises `UsageError` whose message is the usage line of the failing command. Command modules are auto-discovered once per process from the `cli.commands` package with `pkgutil`; discovery never clears commands already registered.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-foundation-executable ===\nIntent: An executable named ledger exists at the application root and an unknown command exits 2 with nothing on stdout.\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\n\npath = \"./ledger\"\nassert os.path.isfile(path)\nassert os.access(path, os.X_OK)\nresult = subprocess.run([path, \"zzunknown\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 2\nassert result.stdout == \"\"\nassert len(result.stderr.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n=== END Zero is written with two decimals and no sign.\n- `write_table(header: list[str], rows: Iterable[Sequence[str]]) -> None` writes the header row then each row to `sys.stdout`, resolved at call time so redirection works. Fields are joined with commas, never quoted, each line ends in `\\n`, and no line has trailing spaces. through `ledger_app.store.open_store` and yields the database.\n\n`tests/test_harness.py::test_temp_database_is_usable` creates an account through the fixture's database and reads it back.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC test-runner-script-shape ===\nIntent: bin/test.sh exists as a POSIX sh script with no modify anything under `sources/`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TextValidation.md ===\n# FEATURE: Text Validation\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Pure validators for codes, names, descriptions, and memos. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | ledger_app.validation.code, ledger_app.validation.name, ledger_app.validation.memo |\n| Consumes | cli.errors |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRules 3 and 4 of the ledger contract: codes (`account`, `category`) are 1 to 12 characters from\n`A-Z` and `0-9`. Names and descriptions are 1 to 40 characters. Memos are 0 to 60 characters. None\nof them contains a comma or a newline.\n\n## Interface\n\nEach function takes a string, returns it unchanged when valid, and raises `cli.errors.Rejected` (exit 3) otherwise.\n\n| Function | Rule |\n|---|---|\n| `code(value)` | Full match of 1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`; a trailing newline is not tolerated. |\n| `name(value)` | 1 to 40 characters with no comma, carriage return, or newline. Also validates descriptions. |\n| `memo(value)` | 0 to 60 characters with no comma, carriage return, or newline. |\n\nValues are never stripped or altered.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC text-validation-code-valid ===\nIntent: Codes of 1 to 12 uppercase letters and digits are returned unchanged.\n\nfrom ledger_app.validation import code\n\nfor value in (\"A\", \"ZZ9\", \"DRAWER1\", \"A\" * 12, \"0123456789AB\"):\n    assert code(value) == value\n=== END AC text-validation-code-valid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-code-invalid ===\nIntent: Empty, over-long, lowercase, punctuated, spaced, comma, and newline codes are rejected with exit code 3.\n\nfrom cli.errors import Rejected\nfrom ledger_app.validation import code\n\nbad = [\"\", \"A\" * 13, \"abc\", \"A-B\", \"A B\", \"A,B\", \"A\\nB\", \"A1\\n\", \"A\\r\"]\nfor value in bad:\n    try:\n        code(value)\n    except Rejected as exc:\n        assert exc.exit_code == 3\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"accepted code \" + repr(value))\n=== END AC text-validation-code-invalid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-name-valid ===\nIntent: Names of 1 to 40 characters without comma or newline are returned unchanged.\n\nfrom ledger_app.validation import name\n\nfor value in (\"F\", \"Front Drawer\", \"N\" * 40, \"Petty Cash 2\"):\n    assert name(value) == value\n=== END AC text-validation-name-valid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-name-invalid ===\nIntent: Empty, over-long, comma, carriage return, and newline names are rejected with exit code 3.\n\nfrom cli.errors import Rejected\nfrom ledger_app.validation import name\n\nbad = [\"\", \"N\" * 41, \"Front, Drawer\", \"Front\\nDrawer\", \"Front\\rDrawer\", \"Front Drawer\\n\"]\nfor value in bad:\n    try:\n        name(value)\n    except Rejected as exc:\n        assert exc.exit_code == 3\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"accepted name \" + repr(value))\n=== END AC text-validation-name-invalid ===\n\n=== AC text-validation-memo ===\nIntent: Memos of 0 to 60 characters pass, and over-long, comma, and newline memos are rejected with exit code 3.\n\nfrom cli.errors import Rejected\nfrom ledger_app.validation import memo\n\nfor value in (\"\", \"walk in sale\", \"M\" * 60):\n    assert memo(value) == value\nbad = [\"M\" * 61, \"a,b\", \"line\\nbreak\", \"line\\rbreak\", \"tape\\n\"]\nfor value in bad:\n    try:\n        memo(value)\n    except Rejected as exc:\n        assert exc.exit_code == 3\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"accepted memo \" + repr(value))\n=== END AC text-validation-memo ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Validators are pure: they read no database, file, or environment.\n- Validators never strip, trim, or otherwise alter a value they accept.\n- Violations are always `Rejected` (exit 3), never a usage or operational error.\n=== END ARTIFACT
